\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{-i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})-\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}{\rm tr}\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big\{\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{+}};F\rangle\langle\sigma_{-}\rvert\langle F\rvert-\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{+}};F\rangle\langle\sigma_{-}\rvert\langle F\rvert\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\big\}\big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times{\rm tr}\big[\lvert\sigma_{-}\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{+}};F\rvert\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})-\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}{\rm tr}\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big\{\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{d\Leftarrow c}\lvert\sigma_{+}\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{-}};F\rvert-\lvert\sigma_{+}\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{-}};F\rvert\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}\big\}\big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times{\rm tr}\big[\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{-}};F\rangle\langle\sigma_{+}\rvert\langle F\rvert\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\big]\Bigg]

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{\langle\sigma_{-}\rvert\langle F\rvert\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}-\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big]\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{+}};F\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{+}};F\rvert\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\lvert\sigma_{-}\rangle\lvert F\rangle}{i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})+\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{-}};F\rvert\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{d\Leftarrow c}-\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big]\lvert\sigma_{+}\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\sigma_{+}\rvert\langle F\rvert\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{\bm{k},-\sigma_{-}};F\rangle}{-i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})+\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\Bigg[\frac{{\rm tr}\Big[\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}-\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big]\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\Big]}{i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})+\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}-\frac{{\rm tr}\Big[\big[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{d\Leftarrow c}-\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\big]\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}\big]}{-i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})+\frac{\kappa_{a}}{2}}\Bigg]

where we have substituted the definition of the Hilbert-Schmidt inner product (Eq. (
S8
)) and the right and left eigenstates of the intermediate states (Eq. (
S116
)) in the first equality,
took the traces in the second,
and rewrote the expression in terms of the trace over the integrand in the third.
The advantage of writing
({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})^{(2{\rm A})}_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}
in this form becomes clear by separating the coefficients to the real and imaginary part as,

\displaystyle({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})^{(2{\rm A})}_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}=-\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{-i(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}{\rm tr}\bigg[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\Big(\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}-\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{\kappa_{a}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \times{\rm tr}\bigg[\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)\dagger}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d}-\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c}\Big)\bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\equiv(\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)},{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2{\rm A})}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)})

(S119)

where we have used the cyclic property of the trace
{\rm tr}[\hat{A}\hat{B}]={\rm tr}[\hat{B}\hat{A}]
.
By further employing
an approximation that the excitation of the conduction electrons occurs near the Fermi surface

\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}\simeq\varepsilon_{\rm F}
,
the effective Lindbladian
{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2{\rm A})}
is simplified
to a GKSL form (Eq. (
S4
)),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2{\rm A})}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i[\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{({\rm A})},\hat{\rho}]+\frac{\kappa_{a}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sum_{\bm{k},\sigma}\hat{V}_{d\Leftarrow c}^{a\bm{k}\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}\Big]\hat{\rho}

(S120)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i[\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{({\rm A})},\hat{\rho}]+\gamma_{a}{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sum_{\bm{k},\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}e^{i\bm{k}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\Big]\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i[\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{({\rm A})},\hat{\rho}]+\gamma_{a}{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sum_{\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}\Big]\hat{\rho}.

Here,
\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}
is a projection operator to a singly-occupied state,

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}=\frac{|v_{a}|^{2}\kappa_{a}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}},

(S121)

is the light-induced dissipation rate, and the Hamiltonian part
\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(A)}
is given by,

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(A)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\frac{-(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}

(S122)

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{-(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}|v_{a}|^{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}\hat{d}^{\dagger}_{\sigma,a}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}.

The remaining two terms of
({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}^{(2)}
in Eq. (
S115
), given by,

\displaystyle({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}^{(2{\rm B})}=\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg[\frac{(\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)},\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{r}_{(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{+})),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}-\hat{r}_{(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{+})),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d})(\hat{l}_{(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{+})),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)},\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)})}{-i(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a})-\frac{\delta_{a}}{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{(\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)},\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{-}))}^{(0)}-\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{-}))}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}\dagger}_{d\Leftarrow c})(\hat{l}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\varnothing;(\bm{k},\sigma_{-}))}^{(0)},\hat{r}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}^{(0)}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}\sigma\dagger}_{c\Leftarrow d})}{i(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a})-\frac{\delta_{a}}{2}}\Bigg],

(S123)

can be similarly computed. We report the result as follows:

\displaystyle({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}^{(2{\rm B})}=\Big(\hat{l}_{n_{l}}^{(0)},{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{\rm(2B)}\hat{r}^{(0)}_{(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)}\Big)=({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}^{\rm(2B)}

(S124)

with

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{\rm(2B)}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i[\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(B)},\hat{\rho}]

(S125)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\pi|v_{a}|^{2}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a})e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg]

and

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(B)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{V}^{a\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}_{d\Leftarrow c}\hat{V}^{a,\bm{k},\sigma}_{c\Leftarrow d}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\approx-\frac{|v_{a}|^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}e^{-i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}^{\dagger}_{\sigma^{\prime}a}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{k}\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}.

(S126)

The second term in Eq. (
S125
) vanishes for the following reasons. The term contains a delta function, which enforces the created conduction electron to have energy at
\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}=\varepsilon_{d,a}
.
This lies below the Fermi surface
\varepsilon_{\rm F}
, which is forbidden due to the Pauli blocking effect.

Adding the two contributions up and reintroducing the sum over the localized electron sites
a
, we obtain
({\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd})_{n_{l},[(\sigma_{+};F),(\sigma_{-};F)]}^{(2)}
, with

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2)}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i[\hat{H}_{\rm eff},\hat{\rho}]+\sum_{a}\gamma_{a}{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sum_{\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\Big]\hat{\rho}.

(S127)

Here, the total Hamiltonian
\hat{H}_{\rm eff}=\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(A)}+\hat{H}_{\rm eff}^{\rm(B)}
.
The Hamiltonian can be rewritten into a sum of two contributions
\hat{H}_{\rm eff}=\hat{H}_{\rm sd}+\hat{H}_{\rm imp}
.
The
sd
Hamiltonian describes the exchange coupling between the conduction electrons and localized spins,

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\rm sd}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}|v_{a}|^{2}\bigg[\frac{\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}\bigg]\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}}e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}\sigma}d^{\dagger}_{\sigma,a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}

(S128)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\frac{g_{a}}{2}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\sum_{i=0}^{3}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}(\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma^{\prime}}\sigma_{i}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a}\sigma})(d^{\dagger}_{\sigma,a}\sigma_{i}^{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a})\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\frac{g_{a}}{2}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}[\bm{\tau}(\bm{R}_{a})\cdot\bm{S}_{a}]\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}+{\rm const.},

where

\displaystyle g_{a}=-|v_{a}|^{2}\bigg[\frac{\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}\bigg](<0)

(S129)

is an antiferromagnetic
sd
coupling,

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{a}=\begin{pmatrix}\hat{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{\dagger}&\hat{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{\dagger}\end{pmatrix}\bm{\sigma}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{d}_{\uparrow,a}\\
\hat{d}_{\downarrow,a}\end{pmatrix}=(S_{a}^{1},S_{a}^{2},S_{a}^{3})

(S130)

is the localized spins (with
\bm{\sigma}=(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3})
being the Pauli matrices), and

\displaystyle\bm{\tau}(\bm{R}_{a})=\begin{pmatrix}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\uparrow}^{\dagger}&\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\downarrow}^{\dagger}\end{pmatrix}\bm{\sigma}\begin{pmatrix}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\uparrow}\\
\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\downarrow}\end{pmatrix}=(\tau_{1}(\bm{R}_{a}),\tau_{2}(\bm{R}_{a}),\tau_{3}(\bm{R}_{a}))

(S131)

is the conduction spin at position
\bm{R}_{a}
.
The impurity Hamiltonian
\hat{H}_{\rm imp}
is given by

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\rm imp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}}|v_{a}|^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}}\sum_{\sigma}e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}\sigma}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}-\sum_{a}\sum_{\bm{k}}|v_{a}|^{2}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}\sum_{\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}^{\dagger}_{\sigma,a}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}

(S132)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\sum_{\sigma}\bigg[g^{c,a}_{\rm imp}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{c}^{\dagger}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}+g_{\rm imp}^{d,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}^{\dagger}_{\sigma,a}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\bigg].

with

\displaystyle g^{c,a}_{\rm imp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-|v_{a}|^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F}}{(\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{a}^{2}}{4}},

(S133)

\displaystyle g_{\rm imp}^{d,a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-|v_{a}|^{2}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}-\varepsilon_{d,a}}.

(S134)

In the equilibrium limit
\kappa_{a}\rightarrow 0
, these reproduce the conventional
sd
Hamiltonian.

A similar calculation can be performed for the contribution for the vacant
\lvert\varnothing\rangle\langle\varnothing\rvert
and double-occupied
\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert
states.
The effective Lindbladian reads, respectively,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2)}(\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a})=\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\pi|v_{a}|^{2}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a})e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\varnothing}\sum_{\bm{q}}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}-\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}-\bm{q},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\varnothing}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg],

(S135)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\rm eff,sd}^{(2)}(\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a})=\kappa_{a}\sum_{\sigma}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}]\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\pi|v_{a}|^{2}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a}-U_{a})e^{i(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}^{\prime},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\kappa_{a}\sum_{\sigma}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}]\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\uparrow\downarrow}\sum_{\bm{q}}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}-\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}-\bm{q},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg].

(S136)

Here, as we are only interested in the dynamics of singly occupied states, we omitted the contribution to the coherent dynamics in the vacant or double-occupied state.

\displaystyle\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\varnothing}=\pi|v_{a}|^{2}\sum_{\bm{k}}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a}),\qquad\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\uparrow\downarrow}=\pi|v_{a}|^{2}\sum_{\bm{k}}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d,a}-U_{a}).

(S137)

are the dissipation rate of the vacant and double-occupied states, and

\bm{k}_{d}
and
\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}
are momenta that satisfies, respectively,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\bm{k}_{d,a}}=\varepsilon_{d,a},\qquad\varepsilon_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}}=\varepsilon_{d,a}+U_{a}.

(S138)

In contrast to the singly occupied state counterparts, (the second term of Eq. (
S125
)), the Pauli blocking effect does not play a role in these terms and therefore they do not vanish.

Summing up all the contributions (Eqs. (
S127
), (
S135
), (
S136
)), we finally obtain,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{\rho}=-i[\hat{H}_{\rm sd}+\hat{H}_{\rm imp},\hat{\rho}]+\sum_{a}\Big[\gamma_{a}{\mathcal{D}}\big[\sum_{\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\big]+\sum_{\sigma}\kappa_{a}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}]\Big]\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\varnothing}\sum_{\bm{q}}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a},\sigma}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}-\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}-\bm{q},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\rm s}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\gamma_{{\rm dis},a,\uparrow\downarrow}\sum_{\bm{q}}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bigg[\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{\rho}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}-\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\{\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U},\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{c}_{\bm{k}_{d,a}^{U}-\bm{q},\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{P}_{\uparrow\downarrow}^{a},\hat{\rho}\}\bigg].

(S139)

In the main text in Eq. (4), we have reported the description without the final two terms, as their role is merely to retain the state in a singly occupied state and they do not play any role in the singly occupied sector we are interested in (but see the remarks below).
We have also dropped the impurity Hamiltonian
\hat{H}_{\rm imp}
.
This completes the derivation of Eq. (4) in the main text.

III.3
Remarks on Eq. (4) in the main text and Eq. (
S139
)

Two remarks are in order.

The first remark is on the condition for justifying the second-order perturbation we employed to derive Eq. (4) in the main text.
The second-order perturbation is justified when its contribution, roughly given by
\sum_{\bm{k}}|v|^{2}/(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d}+U+i\delta)\sim-|v|^{2}/U-i\gamma_{\rm dis}
in the typical case
\varepsilon_{d}\sim\varepsilon_{\rm F}\sim U

(where we have omitted the site index
a
and
\gamma_{\rm dis}\sim\gamma_{{\rm dis},\varnothing},\gamma_{{\rm dis},\uparrow\downarrow}
),
is dominant over the higher-order terms.
The fourth-order contribution (note that the third-order contribution is absent for a similar reason to why the first-order contribution is absent) is roughly estimated as

|v|^{4}(\sum_{\bm{k}}1/(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{d}+U+i\delta)))^{3}

which has the magnitude of the real part

O(|v|^{4}/U^{3},\gamma_{\rm dis}^{3}/|v|^{2})
and
the imaginary part
O((|v|^{2}/U^{2})\gamma_{\rm dis},\gamma_{\rm dis}^{2}/U)
.
By comparing with the second-order contribution, one finds that the second-order perturbation is justified when
|v|/U,\gamma_{\rm dis}/U,\gamma_{\rm dis}/|v|\ll 1
.
Noting that
\gamma_{\rm dis}\sim|v|^{2}/W
(where
W
is the bandwidth), the final condition reads
|v|/W\ll 1
.

This situation for the dissipation rate
\gamma_{\rm dis}
(where it must be small compared to
U
), is in stark contrast to the situation for
\kappa
, where the second-order perturbation is better for larger
\kappa
. This is because the timescale of the intermediate state becomes faster for larger
\kappa
.

Secondly, in the light-induced dissipator in Eq. (
S139
),
the sum over the spin configuration
\sigma=\uparrow,\downarrow
is taken
within
the jump operator,

{\mathcal{D}}[\sum_{\sigma}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}]\hat{\rho}
, instead of having a form
\sum_{\sigma}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}]\hat{\rho}
.
This form is quite crucial to induce tunneling between different spin configurations in a
correlated
manner, giving rise to dissipative coupling between different spin configurations between the conduction and localized spins (the last term in Eq. (5) in the main text).
This can be seen explicitly by operating the dissipator to a singly occupied state.
For example, when the dissipator of this form is applied to the down-spin state, i.e.,
\lvert\downarrow\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert
,
(where we omit the site index
a
for simplicity)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{D}}[(\hat{d}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}+\hat{d}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow})](\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)=(\hat{d}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}+\hat{d}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow})(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)(\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\uparrow}+\hat{c}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\downarrow})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}(\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\uparrow}+\hat{c}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\downarrow})(\hat{d}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}+\hat{d}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow})(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)-\frac{1}{2}(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)(\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\uparrow}+\hat{c}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\downarrow})(\hat{d}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}+\hat{d}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow})

\displaystyle=\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{c,\uparrow}\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{c,\uparrow}\rvert-\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)+\frac{1}{2}\hat{c}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}(\lvert\uparrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert))+\frac{1}{2}(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\uparrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow}

\displaystyle=\lvert\uparrow\downarrow\rangle\lvert\overline{c,\uparrow}\rangle\langle\uparrow\downarrow\rvert\langle\overline{c,\uparrow}\rvert-n(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)+\frac{1}{2}\hat{c}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}(\lvert\uparrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert))+\frac{1}{2}(\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\uparrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)\hat{c}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\downarrow}

(S140)

where
n=(1/2)\langle F\rvert\sum_{\sigma}\hat{c}_{\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\sigma}\lvert F\rangle
and
\lvert\overline{c,\uparrow}\rangle=\hat{c}_{\uparrow}\lvert F\rangle
.
As is clear from the last two terms, this dissipation involves a spin-flip

\displaystyle\lvert\downarrow\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\rightarrow\lvert\uparrow\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert,\lvert\downarrow\rangle\langle\uparrow\rvert.

(S141)

This is in stark different to the case where the sum over the spins
\sigma
is outside the jump operator,

\sum_{\sigma}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\bm{R}_{a},\sigma}]\hat{\rho}
, in which the
\uparrow
and
\downarrow
spins would decay independently. Starting again with the down spin configuration, one finds

\displaystyle\sum_{\sigma}{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\sigma}](\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)={\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\hat{c}_{\uparrow}](\lvert\downarrow\rangle\lvert F\rangle\langle\downarrow\rvert\langle F\rvert)

(S142)

where it is clear that no spin flips are involved.

IV
Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation with light-induced interactions

In the previous section, we have derived an effective low-energy description of the localized electron coupled to conduction electrons (Eq. (4) in the main text or Eq. (
S139
)).
In this section, we derive the equation of motion of the localized spins [Eq. (6) in the main text] interacting through the RKKY interaction
[
36
,
37
,
38
]
) modified by the light injection, by integrating out the conduction electrons, treating them as a non-Markovian bath.

To incorporate the non-Markovian effect arising from the Fermi distribution function of conduction electrons, we employ the Keldysh theory introduced in Sec.
I.2
.
Using Eq. (
S82
),
the Keldysh partition function of Eq. (4) in the main text is given by,

\displaystyle Z=\int{\mathcal{D}}(d,\bar{d}){\mathcal{D}}(c,\bar{c})e^{iS[c,\bar{c},d,\bar{d}]}

(S143)

where the effective action is the sum of three parts:

\displaystyle S[c,\bar{c},d,\bar{d}]=S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{c}^{0}[c,\bar{c}]+S_{\rm sd}[c,\bar{c},d,\bar{d}]

(S144)

Here,
d,\bar{d}
and
c,\bar{c}
are Grassmann variables and

\displaystyle S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{s=\pm}\sum_{a,\sigma}s\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{s}(t)i\partial_{t}d_{\sigma,a}^{s}(t)

(S145)

\displaystyle S_{c}^{0}[c,\bar{c}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{s=\pm}\sum_{\bm{k},\sigma}s\Big[\bar{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)i\partial_{t}c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}\bar{c}_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)\Big],

(S146)

\displaystyle S_{\rm sd}^{\rm coh}[c,\bar{c},d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int dt\sum_{s=\pm}s\sum_{a}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}(-g_{a})e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{s}(t)\bar{c}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{s}(t)c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{s}(t)

(S147)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{s=\pm}s\sum_{a}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}g_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\bar{c}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{s}(t)c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{s}(t)[\delta_{\sigma\sigma^{\prime}}-d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{s}(t_{s\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{s}(t_{-s\delta})]

\displaystyle S_{\rm sd}^{\rm dis}[c,\bar{c},d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int dt\sum_{a}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\gamma_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\Big[\bar{c}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{-}(t)d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t)c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{+}(t)

(S148)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\bar{c}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{+}(t_{+\delta})d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{+}(t_{-\delta})-\frac{1}{2}\bar{c}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\sigma^{\prime}}^{-}(t_{-\delta})d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})c_{\bm{k},\sigma}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\Big]

with
S_{\rm sd}=S_{\rm sd}^{\rm coh}+S_{\rm sd}^{\rm dis}
.

s=\pm
labels the forward and backward contours.
We have added or subtracted an infinitesimal
t_{\pm\delta}=t\pm 0^{+}
to avoid subtleties in normal ordering, see, e.g. Refs.
[
67
,
110
,
100
]
for details.
For the simplicity of notation, we have omitted the projection operator to the singly occupied state and omitted the contribution from the double-occupied state.
The actions involving conduction electrons can be rewritten in a compact form,

\displaystyle S_{c}[d,\bar{d},c,\bar{c}]\equiv S_{c}^{0}[c,\bar{c}]+S_{\rm sd}[d,\bar{d},c,\bar{c}]=\int dt\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}\bar{\Psi}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q}}^{\mathrm{T}}(t)\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\Psi_{\bm{k}}(t)

(S149)

by introducing a spinor-Keldysh representation,

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t)=(\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{0}^{-1}(t))_{\bm{k}}\delta_{\bm{q},\bm{0}}-{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)],\qquad\Psi_{\bm{k}}(t)=\begin{pmatrix}c_{\bm{k},\uparrow}^{+}(t)\\
c_{\bm{k},\downarrow}^{+}(t)\\
c_{\bm{k},\uparrow}^{-}(t)\\
c_{\bm{k},\downarrow}^{-}(t)\end{pmatrix}.

(S150)

Here,

\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{0}^{-1}(t)_{\bm{k}}
is a free Green function in the Keldysh formalism, where its Fourier transform is given by (see Eq. (
S48
))
[
99
]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{0}(\bm{k},\omega)=\begin{pmatrix}G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k},\omega)\bm{1}_{2\times 2}&G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k},\omega)\bm{1}_{2\times 2}\\
G_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k},\omega)\bm{1}_{2\times 2}&G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k},\omega)\bm{1}_{2\times 2}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\big[\frac{1-f_{\bm{k}}}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}+i\delta}+\frac{f_{\bm{k}}}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-i\delta}\big]\bm{1}_{2\times 2}&2\pi i\delta(\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})f_{\bm{k}}\bm{1}_{2\times 2}\\
-2\pi i\delta(\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})(1-f_{\bm{k}})\bm{1}_{2\times 2}&\big[-\frac{f_{\bm{k}}}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}+i\delta}-\frac{1-f_{\bm{k}}}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-i\delta}\big]\bm{1}_{2\times 2}\end{pmatrix},

where
f_{\bm{k}}=[e^{(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-\varepsilon_{\rm F})/(k_{\rm B}T)}+1]^{-1}
is the Fermi distribution function and

\bm{1}_{2\times 2}
is a 2-by-2 identity operator.
The self-energy is given by,

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]&\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{+-}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\\
\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]&\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\end{pmatrix}

(S152)

with

\displaystyle(\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)])_{\sigma^{\prime},\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\bigg[g_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\delta_{\sigma\sigma^{\prime}}-\tilde{g}_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})\bigg],

(S153)

\displaystyle(\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)])_{\sigma^{\prime},\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\bigg[g_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\delta_{\sigma\sigma^{\prime}}-\tilde{g}_{a}^{*}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\bigg],

(S154)

\displaystyle(\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)])_{\sigma^{\prime},\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\sum_{a}\gamma_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}d_{\sigma^{\prime},a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t),

(S155)

\displaystyle(\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{+-}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)])_{\sigma^{\prime},\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0

(S156)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{g}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{a}-i\frac{\gamma_{a}}{2}.

(S157)

Below, for simplicity, we omit the first term in Eqs. (
S153
) and (
S154
)
(which should only quantitatively shift the magnitude of dephasing).

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]=\sum_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\tilde{g}_{a}\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t),

(S158)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]=-\sum_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\tilde{g}_{a}^{*}\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t),

(S159)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]=i\sum_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\gamma_{a}\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t),

(S160)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{+-}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]=0,

(S161)

where

\displaystyle\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}d_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})&d_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})\\
d_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})&d_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})\end{pmatrix},

(S162)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}d_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})&d_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\\
d_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})&d_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\end{pmatrix},

(S163)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}d_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t)&d_{\uparrow,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t)\\
d_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{+}(t)&d_{\downarrow,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{+}(t)\end{pmatrix}.

(S164)

For later use, we Fourier transform these self-energies as,

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{++}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2})[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\delta((\bm{r}_{1}+\bm{r}_{2})/2-\bm{R}_{a})\tilde{g}_{a}\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t)

(S165)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{--}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2})[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\delta((\bm{r}_{1}+\bm{r}_{2})/2-\bm{R}_{a})(-\tilde{g}_{a})\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t),

(S166)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{-+}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2})[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\sum_{a}\gamma_{a}\delta((\bm{r}_{1}+\bm{r}_{2})/2-\bm{R}_{a})\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t),

(S167)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}^{+-}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2})[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0,

(S168)

where

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2},t)=\sum_{\bm{q}}\sum_{\bm{k}}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot(\bm{r}_{1}+\bm{r}_{2})/2}e^{i\bm{k}\cdot(\bm{r}_{1}-\bm{r}_{2})}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t).

(S169)

We now perform the Grassmann integral over the conduction electrons, regarding them as the (non-Markovian) environment for localized electrons, to obtain the effective dynamics of the localized spin dynamics:

\displaystyle Z=\int{\mathcal{D}}(d,\bar{d})e^{iS_{d}[d,\bar{d}]}\int{\mathcal{D}}(c,\bar{c})e^{iS_{c}[d,\bar{d},c,\bar{c}]}\equiv\int{\mathcal{D}}(d,\bar{d})e^{iS_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]}

(S170)

where the effective action
S_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]=S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+\Delta S_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]
reads

\displaystyle e^{i\Delta S_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm det}\Big[(-i)(\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{-1}-\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}])\Big]

(S171)

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle{\rm exp}\Big[-{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]-\frac{1}{2}{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]\Big]+{\rm const.}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\rm exp}\Big[i\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(1)}[d,\bar{d}]+i\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(2)}[d,\bar{d}]\Big]+{\rm const.}

Here, the first-order contribution is

\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(1)}[d,\bar{d}]=i{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]

and the second-order contribution is given by,

\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(2)}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]
,
where
{\rm Tr}[\bm{A}]=\int d\bm{r}_{1}\int d\bm{r}_{2}\int dt_{1}\int dt_{2}{\rm tr}_{\sigma}{\rm tr}_{s}[A(\bm{r}_{1},t_{1};\bm{r}_{2},t_{2})]\delta(\bm{r}_{1}-\bm{r}_{2})\delta(t_{1}-t_{2})

and

{\rm tr}_{\sigma(s)}[\cdots]
describes the trace in the spin (Keldysh) space.

IV.1
First-order correction: Decay of dipole moment

The first-order correction reads

\displaystyle\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(1)}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]=i{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\int dt_{1}\int d\bm{r}_{1}\int dt_{2}\int d\bm{r}_{2}\big[\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2},t_{1})\delta(t_{1}-t_{2})\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{r}_{2}-\bm{r}_{1},t_{2}-t_{1}+\delta)\big]

(S172)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\int dt^{\prime}\sum_{\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}(t^{\prime})\mathbbm{G}_{0}(-\bm{k},t=\delta)\big]=i\int dt\sum_{\bm{k}}\int\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}e^{i\omega s\delta}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}(t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k},\omega)\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\int dt\sum_{\bm{k}}\int\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}e^{i\omega s\delta}{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{++}(t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k},\omega)+\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{--}(t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k},\omega)+\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k},\omega)\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a}\sum_{\sigma}\bigg[-g_{a}\big[d_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})-d_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -i\gamma_{a}n\Big[d_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t)-\frac{1}{2}d_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})-\frac{1}{2}d_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\Big]\bigg]

where we have used

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\omega e^{i\omega\delta}\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k},\omega)=2\pi if_{\bm{k}},

(S173)

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\omega e^{-i\omega\delta}\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k},\omega)=2\pi if_{\bm{k}},

(S174)

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\omega\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k},\omega)=2\pi if_{\bm{k}},

(S175)

and
\sum_{\bm{k}}f_{\bm{k}}=n
, where
n
is the filling.
Comparing this action with
Eq. (
S82
), we find that this corresponds to the equation of motion
\partial_{t}\hat{\rho}={\mathcal{L}}^{(1)}_{\rm eff}\hat{\rho}
governed by a superoperator,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}^{(1)}_{\rm eff}\hat{\rho}=-i\sum_{a}\sum_{\sigma}[\hat{P}_{\rm s}g_{a}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{P}_{\rm s},\hat{\rho}]+\sum_{a}\sum_{\sigma}\gamma_{a}n{\mathcal{D}}[\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}\hat{P}_{\rm s}]\hat{\rho},

(S176)

which are the Hartree energy shift (which we ignore below) and an onsite pumping of localized electrons.
The latter gives rise to a decay of magnetic dipole moment, which can be seen from the equation of motion for the averaged magnetic dipole
\left<\hat{\bm{S}}_{a}\right>={\rm tr}[\hat{\rho}\hat{\bm{S}}_{a}]
,

\displaystyle\left<\dot{S}_{a}^{i}\right>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}[(\partial_{t}\hat{\rho})\hat{S}_{a}^{i}]

(S177)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}{\rm tr}\big[-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}\hat{S}_{a}^{i}-\frac{1}{2}\hat{\rho}\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\hat{S}_{a}^{i}+\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}\Big[-\frac{1}{2}\left<\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\right>-\frac{1}{2}\left<\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\right>+\left<\sum_{\nu}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\right>\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}\Big[-\frac{1}{2}\left<\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\right>-\frac{1}{2}\left<\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\right>+\left<\sum_{\nu}\sum_{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\sigma_{i}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}(\delta_{\mu^{\prime}\nu}-\hat{d}_{\mu^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\nu,a})(\delta_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}-\hat{d}_{\nu,a}^{\dagger}\hat{d}_{\nu^{\prime},a})\right>\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}\Big[-\left<\hat{S}_{a}^{i}\right>+\sum_{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\left<-(\hat{d}_{\mu^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\sigma_{i}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\nu^{\prime},a})-(\hat{d}_{\mu^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\sigma_{i}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\nu^{\prime},a})+2(\hat{d}_{\mu^{\prime},a}^{\dagger}\sigma_{i}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\nu^{\prime},a})\right>\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\gamma_{a}\left<S_{a}^{i}\right>.

IV.2
Second-order correction: RKKY interactions and Gilbert damping

We now move on to the analysis of the second-order perturbation contribution
S_{\rm eff}^{(2)}
, our central interest,
which gives rise to the light-modified RKKY interaction and the Gilbert damping:

\displaystyle\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(2)}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}{\rm Tr}\big[\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}[d,\bar{d}]\mathbbm{G}_{0}\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{2}\int dtdt^{\prime}\int d\bm{r}_{1}d\bm{r}_{2}d\bm{r}_{3}d\bm{r}_{4}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{r}_{1},\bm{r}_{2};t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{r}_{2}-\bm{r}_{3},t-t^{\prime})\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{r}_{3},\bm{r}_{4},t^{\prime})\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{r}_{4}-\bm{r}_{1};t^{\prime}-t)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{2}\int dtdt^{\prime}\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,t-t^{\prime})\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t^{\prime})\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;t^{\prime}-t)\bigg].

(S178)

Recall that the self-energy
\bm{\Sigma}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]
(the Green’s function
{\mathbbm{G}}_{0}
) is composed of (conduction) localized electrons.
The timescale of the dynamics of
\bm{\Sigma}
is therefore much slower than those of
{\mathbbm{G}}_{0}
.
Taking advantage of this property, we perform a gradient expansion as

\displaystyle\Delta S_{\rm eff}^{(2)}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}\int dt_{g}d\tau\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}+\tau/2)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}-\tau/2)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\approx S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\rm Gilbert}[d,\bar{d}].

(S179)

Here, (
\delta\rightarrow 0^{+}
)

\displaystyle S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}\int dt_{g}d\tau\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

(S180)

is the Markovian limit contribution that, as we will show below, gives rise to the RKKY interaction and its correction from the light-induced dissipation.
The first-order non-Markovian correction is given by,

\displaystyle S_{\rm Gilbert}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}\int dt_{g}d\tau\frac{\tau}{2}\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}\Bigg[{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}+\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle-{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}(t_{g}-\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]\Bigg]

which will be shown below to give rise to the Gilbert damping.

IV.2.1
Markovian contribution: RKKY interaction

First, consider the Markovian contribution.
Taking the trace in the Keldysh space and using the property that
\bm{\Sigma}^{+-}=0
,

\displaystyle S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{2}\int dt_{g}\int d\tau\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

(S182)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2,-\tau)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg].

The conduction electron Green’s functions can be written as
[
99
]
,

\displaystyle G_{0}^{++}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})=\theta(t-t^{\prime})G_{0}^{>}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})+\theta(t^{\prime}-t)G_{0}^{<}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime}),

(S183a)

\displaystyle G_{0}^{--}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})=\theta(t^{\prime}-t)G_{0}^{>}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})+\theta(t-t^{\prime})G_{0}^{<}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime}),

(S183b)

\displaystyle G_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})=G_{0}^{<}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime}),

(S183c)

\displaystyle G_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime})=G_{0}^{>}(\bm{k},t-t^{\prime}),

(S183d)

where the lesser (
s=<
) and greater (
s=>
) Green’s function are collectively expressed as (in Fourier space),

\displaystyle G_{0}^{s}(\bm{k},\omega)=F^{s}_{\bm{k}}[G_{0}^{\rm R}(\bm{k},\omega)-G_{0}^{\rm A}(\bm{k},\omega)]

(S184)

with

\displaystyle G_{0}^{\rm R}(\bm{k},\omega)=\frac{1}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}+i\delta},\qquad G_{0}^{\rm A}(\bm{k},\omega)=\frac{1}{\omega-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}-i\delta},

(S185)

\displaystyle F^{<}(\bm{k})=-f_{\bm{k}},\qquad F^{>}(\bm{k})=1-f_{\bm{k}}.

(S186)

Substituting this expression into Eq. (
S182
),
we encounter integrals of the form
(
s_{1},...,s4=+,-
and
s_{a},s_{b}=<,>
),

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\tau\theta(\tau)G_{0}^{s_{a}}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{s_{b}}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta},

(S187)

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\tau\theta(-\tau)G_{0}^{s_{a}}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{s_{b}}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta},

(S188)

where

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\pm}\equiv\varepsilon_{\bm{k}\pm\bm{q}/2},\qquad F_{\pm}^{s}\equiv F^{s}_{\bm{k}\pm\bm{q}/2}.

(S189)

Using these relations, one obtains,

\displaystyle S^{\rm RKKY}_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]\approx-\frac{1}{2}\int dt

\displaystyle\times\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\bigg[\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{-+}(t+\delta)

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)-\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)\bigg]

(S190)

We proceed by plugging in the explicit form of
\Sigma
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}\mp i\delta}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{s_{1}s_{2}}(t\pm\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{s_{3}s_{4}}(t\mp\delta)

\displaystyle=\sum_{a,b}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}\mp i\delta}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot(\bm{R}_{a}-\bm{R}_{b})}\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{s_{1},s_{2}}(t\pm\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{b}^{s_{3},s_{4}}(t\mp\delta)

\displaystyle=\sum_{a,b}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}\mp i\delta}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{s_{1},s_{2}}(t\pm\delta)\bm{\Sigma}_{b}^{s_{3},s_{4}}(t\mp\delta)

(S191)

where
\bm{R}_{a,b}=\bm{R}_{a}-\bm{R}_{b}
and

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{s_{1},s_{2}}(t)=\sum_{a}e^{i\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a}}\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{s_{1},s_{2}}(t)

(S192)

with

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{++}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(g_{a}-i\frac{\gamma_{a}}{2}\Big)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t),

(S193)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{--}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(g_{a}+i\frac{\gamma_{a}}{2}\Big)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t),

(S194)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{-+}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\gamma_{a}\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t),

(S195)

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{+-}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

(S196)

In the final equality of Eq. (
S191
), we have used the property that

\varepsilon_{\pm}\equiv\varepsilon_{\bm{k}\pm\bm{q}/2},F_{\pm}^{s}\equiv F^{s}_{\bm{k}\pm\bm{q}/2}
, and
\bm{\Sigma}_{a}^{s,s^{\prime}}
are symmetric under the transformation

(\bm{k},\bm{q})\rightarrow(-\bm{k},-\bm{q})
.

After a lengthy but straightforward calculation, we arrive at,

\displaystyle S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]=\int dt\sum_{a,b}\bigg[J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle+i\Lambda_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle+[H_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})-i\Gamma_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})]{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t)\big]

\displaystyle+P_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle-i\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle-W_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle-i\Phi_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]-2{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\big]\Big)\bigg]

where

\displaystyle J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big(g_{a}g_{b}-\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{4}\Big)-\frac{1}{2}m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})[(-g_{a})\gamma_{b}+(-g_{b})\gamma_{a}],

(S198)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big(g_{a}g_{b}-\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{4}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b})[(-g_{a})\gamma_{b}+(-g_{b})\gamma_{a}],

(S199)

\displaystyle H_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})-i\Gamma_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big[[(-g_{a})\gamma_{b}-(-g_{b})\gamma_{a}]-2i\Big(g_{a}g_{b}+\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{4}\Big)\Big],

(S200)

\displaystyle P_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{2}+m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\gamma_{a}(-g_{b}),

(S201)

\displaystyle\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\gamma_{a}(-g_{b})-m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{2},

(S202)

\displaystyle W_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})(-g_{a})\gamma_{b},

(S203)

\displaystyle\Phi_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{\gamma_{a}\gamma_{b}}{2}.

(S204)

and

\displaystyle f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}-F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}}=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{(1-f_{+})(-f_{-})-(-f_{+})(1-f_{-}))}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}}

(S205)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\frac{f_{+}-f_{-}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}},

\displaystyle m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\pi\delta(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-})(F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}+F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>})

(S206)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})\pi\delta(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-})((1-f_{+})f_{-}+f_{+}(1-f_{-})),

\displaystyle h_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})2\pi\delta(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-})F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{<}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\cos(\bm{q}\cdot\bm{R}_{a,b})2\pi\delta(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-})f_{+}f_{-}

(S207)

Here,
J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})
is identical to the well-known form of the RKKY interaction in the equilibrium limit
\gamma_{a}=0
.
We note
m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

vanishes at
T=0
.

One can check that this action has the causality structure
S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]|_{d_{+}=d_{-},\bar{d}_{+}=\bar{d}_{-}}=0

[
99
]
, implying the trace conserving property
\partial_{t}{\rm tr}[\hat{\rho}]=0

[
67
]
.

Let us first show that the conventional RKKY interaction
[
36
,
37
,
38
]
recovers in the equilibrium limit
\gamma_{a}=0
.
In this limit, the effective action reads,

\displaystyle S_{\rm eq}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]=\int dt\sum_{a,b}\bigg[J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +ig_{a}g_{b}m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -2ig_{a}g_{b}m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b}){\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t)\big]\bigg].

(S208)

Using the relation,

\displaystyle{\rm tr}_{\sigma}[\mathbbm{D}^{s_{1},s_{2}}_{a}(t)\mathbbm{D}^{s_{3},s_{4}}_{b}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\bigg[\begin{pmatrix}d_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{2}}&d_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{2}}\\
d_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{2}}&d_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{2}}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}d_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{4}}&d_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{4}}\\
d_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{4}}&d_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{4}}\end{pmatrix}\bigg]

(S209)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{2}}d_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{4}}+d_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{2}}d_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{4}}+d_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{2}}d_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{4}}+d_{\downarrow,a}^{s_{1}}\bar{d}_{\uparrow,a}^{s_{2}}d_{\uparrow,b}^{s_{3}}\bar{d}_{\downarrow,b}^{s_{4}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{3}\sum_{\mu,\nu=\uparrow,\downarrow}\sum_{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}=\uparrow,\downarrow}(d_{\nu,a}^{s_{1}}\sigma_{i}^{\mu\nu}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{s_{2}})(d_{\nu^{\prime},b}^{s_{3}}\sigma_{i}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\bar{d}_{\mu^{\prime},b}^{s_{4}}),

we get

\displaystyle S^{\rm RKKY}_{\rm eq}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,b}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\bigg[\frac{J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\sum_{s=\pm}s\hat{m}_{a,j}^{s,s}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{s,s}

(S210)

\displaystyle+\frac{i}{2}g_{a}g_{b}m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big[\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,+}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{+,+}+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{-,-}-2\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{+,+}\Big]\bigg]

where (
l_{1},l_{2}=+,-
)

\displaystyle\hat{m}_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}[d,\bar{d}]=\sum_{\mu,\nu=\uparrow,\downarrow}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{l_{1}}\hat{\sigma}_{j}^{\mu\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{l_{2}}.

(S211)

We find by comparing with Eq. (
S82
) that the corresponding Liouville equation is,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{\rho}=-i[\hat{H}_{\rm RKKY},\hat{\rho}]+\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\mathcal{D}}\Big[\sum_{a}g_{a}\sqrt{m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}\hat{S}_{a,j}\Big]\hat{\rho}

(S212)

with

\hat{H}_{\rm RKKY}=\sum_{a,b}(J_{a,b}/2)\bm{\hat{S}}_{a}\cdot\bm{\hat{S}}_{b}

and
(\hat{\bm{S}}_{a})_{j}=\sum_{\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\sigma,a}^{\dagger}(\sigma_{j})_{\sigma\sigma^{\prime}}\hat{d}_{\sigma^{\prime},a}
is the localized spin operator.
This is identical to those found in Ref.
[
111
]
,
where the dissipator describes the decoherence effect on the spins that become non-zero at finite temperature
T>0
.

Having checked that our formalism reproduces the known results in the equilibrium limit,
we now add back the light-induced terms.
For simplicity, we restrict ourselves to
T=0
and the case
\gamma_{a}\ll|g_{a}|
, corresponding to the case where
\kappa_{a}\ll U_{a}
.
Noting that
m_{\rm dis}(\bm{R}_{a,b})
vanishes at the zero temperature limit, the effective Keldysh action simplifies to

\displaystyle S_{\rm eff}^{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]=\int dt\sum_{a,b}\bigg[J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

(S213)

\displaystyle+i\frac{\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})+\Omega_{ba}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle-i\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{++}(t-\delta)\big]+{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{--}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{-+}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)

\displaystyle-W_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})\Big({\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{++}(t+\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\big]-{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\mathbbm{D}_{b}^{-+}(t-\delta)\mathbbm{D}_{a}^{--}(t+\delta)\big]\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\sum_{s=\pm}s\hat{m}_{a,j}^{s,s}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{s,s}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle i\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\Big[\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,+}m_{b,j}^{+,+}+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}m_{b,j}^{-,-}-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,-}m_{b,j}^{+,+}-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}m_{b,j}^{+,-}\Big]

where (note that
g_{a}<0
)

\displaystyle J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{a}g_{b}f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b}),

(S214)

\displaystyle\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{a}|g_{b}|f_{\rm RKKY}(\bm{R}_{a,b}).

(S215)

In the final equality, we have ignored the contribution arising from the term proportional to
W_{a,b}
since they only contribute to the coherent motion,
which is subdominant
o(J_{a,b})
in the regime of interest
\kappa_{a}\ll U_{a}
(or
\gamma_{a}\ll|g_{a}|)
.

IV.2.2
Non-Markovian correction: Gilbert damping

We next analyze the non-Markovian correction
S^{\rm Gilbert}_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]
.
Taking the trace over the Keldysh space,

\displaystyle S_{\rm Gilbert}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}\int dt_{g}d\tau\frac{\tau}{2}\sum_{\bm{q},\bm{k}}\Bigg[{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{++}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{-+}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t_{g}-\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{+-}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -{\rm tr}_{\sigma,s}\bigg[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}+\delta)\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)[\partial_{t_{g}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t_{g}-\delta)]\mathbbm{G}_{0}^{--}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)\bigg]\Bigg]

Here, we have omitted the contribution from the light-induced dissipation by setting
\Sigma^{-+}=0
, assuming that they are small compared to those already present in their absence.
Similarly to the case of Markovian contribution
S^{\rm RKKY}_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]
,
since the conduction electron Green’s functions can be split into a sum over lesser and greater Green’s function (Eq. (
S183
)),
we encounter forms like

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\tau\tau\theta(\tau)G_{0}^{s_{a}}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{s_{b}}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}},

(S217)

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}d\tau\tau\theta(-\tau)G_{0}^{s_{a}}(\bm{k}+\bm{q}/2,\tau)G_{0}^{s_{b}}(\bm{k}-\bm{q}/2;-\tau)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{F_{+}^{s_{a}}F_{-}^{s_{b}}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta)^{2}}.

(S218)

Using these relations, we obtain,

\displaystyle S^{\rm Gilbert}_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]=\frac{i}{2}\int dt

\displaystyle\times\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\bigg[\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)]\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta)^{2}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)]

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)]\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)+\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta)^{2}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)]

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)]\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)+\frac{F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta)^{2}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)]

\displaystyle+\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)]\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)+\frac{F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}+i\delta)^{2}}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}+\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k}-\bm{q},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)]\bigg].

(S219)

Let us further ignore the non-local contribution
[
68
,
69
,
70
]
for simplicity, by taking into account contribution only from
\bm{q}=0
in the self-energy, i.e.,
\Sigma_{\bm{k},\bm{k}\pm\bm{q}}\approx\Sigma_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}
.
In this case, using
\int dt{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{++}(t+\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{++}(t-\delta)]\big]=\int dt{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{--}(t-\delta)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{--}(t+\delta)]\big]=0
, the effective action simplifies to

\displaystyle S^{\rm Gilbert}_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]=\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}[F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}-F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}]{\rm Im}\bigg[\frac{1}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}\bigg]\int dt\Big[{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{++}(t)[\partial_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{k},\bm{k}}^{--}(t)]\big]\Big]

\displaystyle=-\sum_{a}\frac{\alpha_{a}}{2}\int dt\Big[{\rm tr}_{\sigma}\big[{\mathbbm{D}}_{a}^{++}(t)[\partial_{t}{\mathbbm{D}}_{a}^{--}(t)]\big]\Big]

\displaystyle=-\sum_{a}\frac{\alpha_{a}}{4}\int dt\sum_{j=0}^{3}(\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{+}\sigma_{j}^{\mu\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{+})[\partial_{t}(\bar{d}_{\mu^{\prime},a}^{-}\sigma_{j}^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}d_{\nu^{\prime},a}^{-})]

(S220)

where

\displaystyle\alpha_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2g_{a}^{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}[F_{+}^{<}F_{-}^{>}-F_{+}^{>}F_{-}^{<}]{\rm Im}\bigg[\frac{1}{(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-i\delta)^{2}}\bigg]=-4\pi g_{a}^{2}\sum_{\bm{k},\bm{q}}\frac{f_{+}-f_{-}}{\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}}\delta(\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-})

(S221)

For parabolic dispersion
\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}=\bm{k}^{2}/(2m)
at three spatial dimensions, one obtains the form,

\displaystyle\alpha_{a}=-4\pi g_{a}^{2}\frac{2\pi(V/N)^{2}}{(4\pi^{2})^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}dkk^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}dqq^{2}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}d\theta\cos\theta\frac{f_{\bm{k}+\bm{q}}-f_{\bm{k}}}{\varepsilon_{\bm{k}+\bm{q}}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}}}\delta(\varepsilon_{\bm{k}+\bm{q}}-\varepsilon_{\bm{k}})

\displaystyle=\frac{9\pi^{2}}{2}\frac{n_{e}^{2}}{(N/V)^{2}}\frac{g_{a}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}^{2}}=\frac{9\pi^{2}}{2}n^{2}\frac{g_{a}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\rm F}^{2}}

(S222)

where
n_{e}=k_{\rm F}^{3}/(3\pi^{2})
is the electron density
(where
k_{\rm F}
is the Fermi momentum),
N
is the number of sites,
V
is the volume, and

n=n_{e}/(N/V)
is the filling of the conduction electrons.

IV.3
Semiclassical approximation: Derivation of Eq. (6) in the main text

Summarizing all terms derived above, the Keldysh action
Z=\int{\mathcal{D}}[d,\bar{d}]e^{iS_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]}
reads,

\displaystyle S_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]=S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\gamma}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{M}[d,\bar{d}]

(S223)

with

\displaystyle S_{M}[d,\bar{d}]=S^{\rm coh}_{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\rm Gilbert}[d,\bar{d}]+S^{\rm neq}_{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}].

(S224)

Here,

\displaystyle S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\gamma}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,\sigma}\bigg[\Big[\sum_{s=\pm}s\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{s}i\partial_{t}d_{\sigma,a}^{s}\Big]+i\gamma_{a}n\big[d_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t)\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t)-\frac{1}{2}d_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{+\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{+}(t_{-\delta})-\frac{1}{2}d_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{-\delta})\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{-}(t_{+\delta})\big]\bigg]

(S225)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,\sigma}\Big[\big[\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{q}i\partial_{t}d_{\sigma,a}^{c}+\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{c}i\partial_{t}d_{\sigma,a}^{q}\big]-i\gamma_{a}n\big[\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{q}d_{\sigma,a}^{q}+\frac{1}{2}\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{q}d_{\sigma,a}^{c}-\frac{1}{2}\bar{d}_{\sigma,a}^{c}d_{\sigma,a}^{q}\big]\Big]

\displaystyle S^{\rm coh}_{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\sum_{s=\pm}s\hat{m}_{a,j}^{s,s}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{s,s}

(S226)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{J_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\Big[(\hat{m}_{a,j}^{cq}+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{qc})(\hat{m}_{b,j}^{cc}+\hat{m}_{b,j}^{qq})\Big],

\displaystyle S_{\rm Gilbert}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\frac{\alpha_{a}}{4}\int dt\sum_{j=0}^{3}\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,+}(t)\partial_{t}\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}(t)

(S227)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{a}\frac{\alpha_{a}}{4}\int dt\sum_{j=0}^{3}\big[\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,q}(t)+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,c}(t)\big]\partial_{t}\big[\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,c}(t)+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,q}(t)\big]

\displaystyle S^{\rm neq}_{\rm RKKY}[d,\bar{d}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\Big[\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,+}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{+,+}+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{-,-}-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{+,-}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{+,+}-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{-,-}\hat{m}_{b,j}^{+,-}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle i\int dt\sum_{a,b}\frac{\Omega_{a,b}(\bm{R}_{a,b})}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\Big[(\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,c}-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,q}+2\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,q})(\hat{m}_{b,j}^{c,c}+\hat{m}_{b,j}^{q,q})+(\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,q}+\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,c})(\hat{m}_{b,j}^{c,q}+\hat{m}_{b,j}^{q,c})\Big]

where
we have introduced the classical-quantum representation,

\displaystyle d_{\mu,a}^{c}(t)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(d_{\mu,a}^{+}(t)+d_{\mu,a}^{-}(t)),\qquad d_{\mu,a}^{q}(t)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(d_{\mu,a}^{+}(t)-d_{\mu,a}^{-}(t)),

(S229)

and (
l_{1},l_{2}=+,-
or
c,q
)

\displaystyle\hat{m}_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}[d,\bar{d}]=\sum_{\mu,\nu=\uparrow,\downarrow}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{l_{1}}\hat{\sigma}_{j}^{\mu\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{l_{2}}.

(S230)

In the following, we will perform a saddle point approximation to obtain a semiclassical description of spins, namely the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation modified to exhibit non-reciprocal interactions (Eq. (6) in the main text).
For this purpose, we introduce a set of auxiliary fields
m
and Lagrange multipliers
\lambda
as

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int{\mathcal{D}}[d,\bar{d}]e^{iS_{\rm eff}[d,\bar{d}]}=\int{\mathcal{D}}[d,\bar{d}]e^{i(S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\gamma}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{M}[\hat{m}[d,\bar{d}]])}

(S231)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int{\mathcal{D}}[m]\int{\mathcal{D}}[d,\bar{d}]\delta(\hat{m}[d,\bar{d}]-m)e^{i(S_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{\gamma}[d,\bar{d}]+S_{M}[m])}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int{\mathcal{D}}[m]e^{iS_{M}[m]}\int{\mathcal{D}}[\lambda]\int{\mathcal{D}}[d,\bar{d}]e^{iS_{d}^{0}[d,\bar{d}]+iS_{\gamma}[d,\bar{d}]}e^{iS_{\lambda}[\lambda,m,\hat{m}[d,\bar{d}]]}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\int{\mathcal{D}}[m]e^{iS_{M}[m]}e^{iS_{B}[m]}

(S232)

where

\displaystyle S_{\lambda}[\lambda,m,\hat{m}[d,\bar{d}]]=\int dt\sum_{a}\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}=q,c}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\lambda_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}(t)\big[m_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}(t)-\hat{m}_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\big]

(S233)

with

\displaystyle\bm{\lambda}_{a}(t)=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{q,q}(t)&\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{q,c}(t)\\
\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{c,q}(t)&\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{c,c}(t)\end{pmatrix},\qquad\bm{m}_{a}(t)=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{m}_{a}^{q,q}(t)&\bm{m}_{a}^{q,c}(t)\\
\bm{m}_{a}^{c,q}(t)&\bm{m}_{a}^{c,c}(t)\end{pmatrix},\qquad\bm{\hat{m}}_{a}(t)=\begin{pmatrix}\hat{\bm{m}}_{a}^{q,q}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]&\hat{\bm{m}}_{a}^{q,c}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\\
\hat{\bm{m}}_{a}^{c,q}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]&\hat{\bm{m}}_{a}^{c,c}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\end{pmatrix}.

Note that
\bm{m}_{a}^{c,q}(t)=[\bm{m}_{a}^{c,q}(t)]^{*}
and
\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{c,q}(t)=[\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{q,c}(t)]^{*}
,
where

\bm{m}_{a}^{l_{1},l_{2}}=(m_{a,1}^{l_{1},l_{2}},m_{a,2}^{l_{1},l_{2}},m_{a,3}^{l_{1},l_{2}})

and

\bm{\lambda}_{a}^{l_{1},l_{2}}=(\lambda_{a,1}^{l_{1},l_{2}},\lambda_{a,2}^{l_{1},l_{2}},\lambda_{a,3}^{l_{1},l_{2}})
.
Here, the idea is to replace all
\hat{m}
’s composed of a product of Grassmann variables with real numbers
m
.

The physical meaning of
\bm{m}_{a}^{l_{1},l_{2}}(t)
become clear by
taking the saddle point of Eq. (
S231
) in terms of
\lambda
as
0=\delta S_{\lambda}[\lambda,m,\hat{m}[d,\bar{d}]]/\delta\lambda_{a,j}^{l_{1},l_{2}}
, or

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}m_{a,j}^{q,q}(t)&m_{a,j}^{q,c}(t)\\
m_{a,j}^{c,q}(t)&m_{a,j}^{c,c}(t)\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\left<\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,q}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\right>&\left<\hat{m}_{a,j}^{q,c}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\right>\\
\left<\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,q}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\right>&\left<\hat{m}_{a,j}^{c,c}[d(t),\bar{d}(t)]\right>\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\left<\sum_{\mu,\nu}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{q}(t)\sigma_{j}^{\mu,\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{q}(t)\right>&\left<\sum_{\mu,\nu}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{q}(t)\sigma_{j}^{\mu,\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{c}(t)\right>\\
\left<\sum_{\mu,\nu}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{c}(t)\sigma_{j}^{\mu,\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{q}(t)\right>&\left<\sum_{\mu,\nu}\bar{d}_{\mu,a}^{c}(t)\sigma_{j}^{\mu,\nu}d_{\nu,a}^{c}(t)\right>\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&\theta(t-t^{\prime})\left<\sum_{\mu,\nu}\sigma_{j}^{\mu,\nu}\{\hat{d}_{\mu,a}^{\dagger}(t),\hat{d}_{\nu,a}(t^{\prime})\}\right>\big|_{t^{\prime}\rightarrow t+\delta}\\
